*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM GET_EXIT_TITLES                                           *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       returns name and title of all available standard-exits         *
*       every exit in this formpool has to be added to this form.      *
*       You have to specify a parameter type in order to enable the    *
*       code generation program to determine correctly how to          *
*       generate the user exit call, i.e. how many and what kind of    *
*       parameter(s) are used in the user exit.                        *
*       The following parameter types exist:                           *
*                                                                      *
*       TYPE                Description              Usage             *
*    ------------------------------------------------------------      *
*       C_EXIT_PARAM_NONE   Use no parameter         Subst. and Valid. *
*                           except B_RESULT                            *
*       C_EXIT_PARAM_CLASS  Use a type as parameter  Subst. and Valid  *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  EXIT_TAB  table with exit-name and exit-titles                 *
*                 structure: NAME(5), PARAM(1), TITEL(60)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM get_exit_titles TABLES etab.

  DATA: BEGIN OF exits OCCURS 50,
          name(5)   TYPE c,
          param     LIKE c_exit_param_none,
          title(60) TYPE c,
        END OF exits.

  exits-name  = 'U001'.
  exits-param = c_exit_param_none.
  exits-title = text-001.              "Posting date check
  APPEND exits.
* forms for SAP_EIS
  exits-name  = 'US001'.               "single validation: only one
  exits-param = c_exit_param_none.     "data record used
  exits-title = text-102.              "Example EIS
  exits-title = text-102.              "Example EIS
  APPEND exits.
*
  exits-name  = 'U102'.                "single validation: only one
  exits-param = c_exit_param_none.     "data record used
  exits-title = text-102.              "Example EIS
  APPEND exits.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule                          *
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.        *

**eject
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**       FORM U101
*
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**       Example of an exit using the complete data from one
*
**       multi-line rule.
*
**       This exit is intended for use from callup point 3, in FI.
*
**
*
**       If account 400000 is used, then account 399999 must be posted
*
**       to in another posting line.
*
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**  -->  BOOL_DATA   The complete posting data.
*
**  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
*
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
*
*********Added by anjali at 16.01.2018
*FORM ZPOST CHANGING B_RESULT.
*
*
*  TABLES : ZFI_POST.
*  DATA : LT_BKPF TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF BKPF,
*         LS_BKPF TYPE BKPF.
*
*  DATA : LT_BSIS TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF BSIS,
*         LS_BSIS TYPE BSIS.
*
*  DATA : LT_POST TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ZFI_POST,
*         LS_POST TYPE ZFI_POST.
*
*  DATA : LT_REVERSE TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ZFI_REVERSE,
*         LS_REVERSE TYPE ZFI_REVERSE.
*
*  SELECT SINGLE *
*    FROM ZFI_REVERSE
*    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF LS_REVERSE
*    WHERE ZBELNR = BKPF-STBLG.
*  DATA : LV_DATE TYPE DATUM.
*  LV_DATE = LS_REVERSE-ZCPUDT + 3.
*  IF LS_REVERSE-ZDAY = '00' OR LV_DATE = SY-DATUM.
*  ELSEIF LS_REVERSE-ZDAY = '03'.
*    MESSAGE 'Document does not reverse today' TYPE 'E'.
*  ENDIF.
**
**  SELECT *
**    FROM ZFI_POST
**    INTO TABLE LT_POST
**    WHERE BELNR = BKPF-BELNR.
**
**  SELECT *
**    FROM BSIS
**    INTO TABLE LT_BSIS
**    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN LT_POST
**    WHERE BELNR = LT_POST-BELNR.
**
**  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
**    LOOP AT LT_POST INTO LS_POST.
**      MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_POST TO ZFI_POST.
**      ZFI_POST-BELNR = BKPF-BELNR.
**      ZFI_POST-BUDAT = BKPF-BUDAT.
**      ZFI_POST-GJAHR = BKPF-GJAHR.
**      ZFI_POST-REV_DOC = BKPF-STBLG.
**      ZFI_POST-UPDFLAG = 'X'.
**      MODIFY ZFI_POST.
**    ENDLOOP.
**    LOOP AT LT_BSIS INTO LS_BSIS.
**      MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_BSIS TO ZFI_POST.
**      ZFI_POST-HKONT = LS_BSIS-HKONT.
**      MODIFY ZFI_POST.
**    ENDLOOP.
**
**  ENDIF.

*
*
*
*ENDFORM.
**********end by anjali*******

FORM u101 USING    bool_data TYPE gb002_015
          CHANGING b_result.


ENDFORM.                                                    "u101

**eject
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

*       FORM US001
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule in SAP-EIS
*       for aspect 001 (single validation).
*       one data record is transfered in structure CF<asspect>
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*       Attention: for any FORM one has to make an entry in the
*       form GET_EXIT_TITLES at the beginning of this include
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
*--Changes done for composite role on 27.12.2006  Id +003 By S.R.Sudha
*-----+004 changes  for avoiding time taken during payroll operations
*----on 30.4.2007  By CAB_SUDHA
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM us001 USING b_result.

*--------------------- Changes   for all roles ------------------------*


  CONCATENATE 'C:FI_PCS_E0' bkpf-bukrs INTO nprf0.
  CONCATENATE 'C:FI_PCS_E1' bkpf-bukrs INTO nprf1.
  CONCATENATE 'C:FI_PCS_E2' bkpf-bukrs INTO nprf2.
  CONCATENATE 'C:FI_PCS_E3' bkpf-bukrs INTO nprf2.

  SELECT * INTO TABLE ist_nrole FROM agr_users WHERE uname = sy-uname
                                      AND agr_name LIKE 'C:FI_PCS_E%'.

*  SELECT * from  ZFI_OVL_BDP INTO TABLE @data(it_bdp)
*    WHERE AGR_NAME_POST like 'C:FI_PCS_E%'.


  CONCATENATE 'FI_PCS_E0_' bkpf-bukrs '_YY' INTO prf0.
  CONCATENATE 'FI_PCS_E1_' bkpf-bukrs '_YY' INTO prf1.
  CONCATENATE 'FI_PCS_E2_' bkpf-bukrs '_YY' INTO prf2.
  CONCATENATE 'FI_PCS_E3_' bkpf-bukrs '_YY' INTO prf3.

  SELECT * INTO TABLE ist_role FROM agr_users WHERE uname = sy-uname
                                     AND agr_name LIKE 'FI_PCS_E%' .

*    SELECT * from  ZFI_OVL_BDP APPENDING TABLE it_bdp
*    WHERE AGR_NAME_POST like 'FI_PCS_E%'.


  APPEND LINES OF ist_nrole TO ist_role.
*--------------------------------------------end +003 on 27.12.2006
  IF bseg-shkzg  = 'S'.
    READ TABLE l_lbseg WITH KEY buzei = bseg-buzei.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      l_lbseg = bseg.
      APPEND l_lbseg.
    ELSE.
      l_lbseg = bseg.
      MODIFY l_lbseg INDEX sy-tabix.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT ist_role .
    IF  ( ist_role-agr_name CS prf0 OR ist_role-agr_name CS prf1 OR
          ist_role-agr_name CS prf2 OR ist_role-agr_name CS prf3 OR
          ist_role-agr_name CS prf4 OR ist_role-agr_name CS prf5 OR
          ist_role-agr_name CS prf6 OR ist_role-agr_name CS prf7
*-----------------------------------------start+004  on 1.5.2007
          OR

  DATA : BEGIN OF ist_blart_nolimit OCCURS 0,
           blart LIKE bkpf-blart,
         END OF ist_blart_nolimit.

  DATA : BEGIN OF ist_blart_limit10 OCCURS 0,
           blart LIKE bkpf-blart,
         END OF ist_blart_limit10.

  DATA : ist_setleaf1 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf .
  DATA : wa_ist_setleaf1 TYPE  setleaf.
  DATA : ist_setleaf2 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf .
  DATA : wa_ist_setleaf2 TYPE  setleaf.

  SELECT * FROM  setleaf
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf1
WHERE  setname  = 'BDP_DOCTYPE_NOLIMIT'   " Doc types for DEP RUN etc, No financial limits
AND  setclass = '0000'.

  REFRESH ist_blart_nolimit.
  LOOP AT ist_setleaf1 INTO wa_ist_setleaf1 .
    ist_blart_nolimit-blart = wa_ist_setleaf1-valfrom.
    APPEND ist_blart_nolimit.
    CLEAR wa_ist_setleaf2.
  ENDLOOP.

  SELECT * FROM  setleaf
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf2
    WHERE  setname  = 'BDP_DOCTYPE_LIMIT_10LAKH'   " KM type
      AND  setclass = '0000'.

  REFRESH ist_blart_limit10.
  LOOP AT ist_setleaf2 INTO wa_ist_setleaf2 .
    ist_blart_limit10-blart = wa_ist_setleaf2-valfrom.
    APPEND ist_blart_limit10.
    CLEAR wa_ist_setleaf2.
  ENDLOOP.

**1 Check Doc-rype, if Exempted Doc type found, No limit check required.
  READ TABLE ist_blart_nolimit WITH KEY blart = bkpf-blart.
  IF sy-subrc = 0. " No limit check

  ELSE.            " Check limits

**2. cash and bank
**End RD1K995326 CAB_ALOK KChanges in Financial Powers in New BDP - CR 30012165

FORM u001  USING b_result.

  IF bseg-umskz BETWEEN '1' AND '9'  AND bseg-umskz NE '7'.

    CHECK bseg-lifnr NE space.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM lfa1 WHERE lifnr = bseg-lifnr
AND ktokk = 'VEMP'.                                                                      .
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      CONCATENATE 'KSONGC' lfa1-datlt INTO cstctr.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*      SELECT SINGLE * FROM fmzuob WHERE objnr = cstctr.
 SELECT * FROM FMZUOB   UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE OBJNR = CSTCTR  ORDER BY
OBJNR KSTAR ABPER ABGJR .   ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
      CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
      IF bseg-fistl NE fmzuob-fistl .
        SET PARAMETER ID 'FIS' FIELD fmzuob-fistl.
        MESSAGE e157(za) WITH fmzuob-fistl.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* validation for IUT manager                                           *
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.        *

FORM u125 USING b_result.

  cc1 = sy-uname+0(3).
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' 'ONGC' '_IUT' INTO profl.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM usr04 WHERE bname = sy-uname.
  CASE bseg-buzei.                     "LINE ITEM NUMBER
    WHEN '001'.
      IF bkpf-bukrs <> cc1 AND usr04-profs NS profl.
        MESSAGE e152(zfi).
      ENDIF.
    WHEN OTHERS.
      IF bkpf-bukrs <> cc1.            "check for cross-company code.
        IF usr04-profs CS profl.       "check for profile.
* valid G/L A/C in which automatic IUT posting is possile *
          IF bseg-hkont = '0000120111' OR "check for valid account
             bseg-hkont = '0000120112' OR

             bseg-hkont = '0000120116' OR                   "27.11.2001

             bseg-hkont = '0000120118' OR                   "27.11.2001

             bseg-hkont = '0000090602' OR
             bseg-hkont = '0000090603' OR

             bseg-hkont = '0000090691' OR

             bseg-saknr = '0000090602' OR

**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**       FORM U126
*
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**                                       *
**       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.
*
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
*
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*
FORM u126 USING b_result.
  CLEAR:p_budat, w_gjahr.
  IF  NOT bseg-kblnr  IS  INITIAL.
    IF NOT bseg-kblpos  IS INITIAL.
      SELECT  SINGLE  fdatk INTO  w_fdatk FROM kblp WHERE
                                  belnr = bseg-kblnr
                              AND blpos  = bseg-kblpos.

      p_budat = bkpf-budat.
      IF  sy-subrc = 0 .
        PERFORM  getfiscalyear.
        PERFORM fisyearrange.
        IF NOT w_fdatk IN w_duedate.
          b_result = b_false.
          MESSAGE  e155(zfi).
        ELSE.
          b_result = b_true.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "u126

*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*       FORM US105
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM us105 USING b_result.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_FMN' INTO prf0.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_XCC' INTO cash.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_XCB' INTO bank.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_AES' INTO prfaes.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' 'ONGC' '_IUT' INTO profl.
  IF NOT ( bseg-saknr IS INITIAL ) .
    account = bseg-saknr .
  ELSE.
    account = bseg-hkont.
  ENDIF.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM usr04 WHERE  bname = sy-uname.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND  usr04-profs NS '&' .
** CHANGES BY RHI ON 17/02/00 TO INCORPORATE IUT PROFILE
    CASE account .
      WHEN '0000091240' OR '0000091260'. "checking for Fundsmgr(AP)
        IF usr04-profs NS prf0.
          MESSAGE e149(zfi).
        ELSE.
          b_result = b_true .
        ENDIF.
      WHEN '0000091201' .              " checking for cash account
        IF  usr04-profs CS cash .
          b_result = b_true .
        ELSEIF
           usr04-profs CS bank .
          b_result = b_true .
        ELSE.
          MESSAGE e150(zfi).

        ENDIF.
      WHEN '0000091212' .              " checking for bank accounts
        IF  usr04-profs CS bank .
          b_result = b_true .
        ELSEIF
          usr04-profs CS prfaes .                           " or
          b_result = b_true .
        ELSE.
          MESSAGE e151(zfi).

        ENDIF.
      WHEN  '0000091242'.              " checking for bank accounts
        IF  usr04-profs CS prfaes .
          b_result = b_true .
        ELSEIF
          usr04-profs CS bank .                             " or
          b_result = b_true .
        ELSE.
          MESSAGE e151(zfi).
        ENDIF.
    ENDCASE.
  ELSE.
    b_result = b_true .
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "us105

**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*       FORM US106
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM us106 USING b_result.

  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_FMN' INTO prf0.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_XCC' INTO cash.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_XCB' INTO bank.
  CONCATENATE 'Z:' bkpf-bukrs '_AES' INTO prfaes.
  IF NOT ( bseg-saknr IS INITIAL ) .
    account = bseg-saknr .
  ELSE.
    account = bseg-hkont.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM usr04 WHERE  bname = sy-uname.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND  usr04-profs NS '&'  .

    IF account = '0000091240' OR
       account = '0000091260'.
      IF usr04-profs NS prf0.
        MESSAGE e141(zfi).
      ELSE.
        b_result = b_true.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM get_setname USING account CHANGING setname.
      CASE setname.
        WHEN 'CASH_ACCOUNTS'.  "'0000091201'. "checking for cashacct
          MESSAGE e142(zfi).
        WHEN 'BANK_CLG_ACCOUNTS'.  "'0000091212'. "checking for bank acc
          IF  usr04-profs CS bank .
            b_result = b_true .
          ELSEIF
            usr04-profs CS prfaes .                         " or
            b_result = b_true .
          ELSE.
            MESSAGE e143(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        WHEN OTHERS .
          b_result = b_true .
      ENDCASE.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    b_result = b_true .
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "us106

**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*       FORM UF103
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
*    VALIDATION FOR DOCUMENT TYPES CP, CR, BP, BR AND SA
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 3
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM uf103 USING    bool_data TYPE gb002_015
           CHANGING b_result.
  DATA : flg_nohkont,
         sum_debit   LIKE bseg-wrbtr,
         sum_credit  LIKE bseg-wrbtr,
         sum_diff    LIKE bseg-wrbtr.

  b_result = b_true.
  CLEAR : sum_debit,
          sum_credit.

  CASE bkpf-blart.

    WHEN 'CP' OR 'CR'.
      LOOP AT bool_data-bseg INTO bseg.

        CLEAR flg_nohkont.

        SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE  setname  = 'CASH_ACCOUNTS' ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
          IF bseg-hkont BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
            CLEAR flg_nohkont.
            EXIT.
          ELSE.
            flg_nohkont = 'X'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDSELECT.

* For accounts common to Cash and Bank (91299) no check reqd
        SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE  setname  = 'BANK_CLG_ACCOUNTS'.
          IF bseg-hkont BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
            flg_nohkont = 'X'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDSELECT.

        IF flg_nohkont = 'X'.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        IF bseg-shkzg = 'H'.           " Credit
          sum_credit = sum_credit + bseg-wrbtr.
        ELSEIF bseg-shkzg = 'S'.       "Debit.
          sum_debit  = sum_debit  + bseg-wrbtr.
        ENDIF.

      ENDLOOP.

      IF bkpf-blart = 'CP' AND ( sum_credit < sum_debit ).
        b_result = b_false.
      ENDIF.

      IF bkpf-blart = 'CR' AND ( sum_credit > sum_debit ).
        b_result = b_false.
      ENDIF.

* added on 150401 to prevent CP CR postings without cash accounts

      IF sum_credit = 0 AND sum_debit = 0.
        b_result = b_false.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'BP' OR 'BR'.
      LOOP AT bool_data-bseg INTO bseg.

        CLEAR flg_nohkont.

        SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE  setname  = 'BANK_CLG_ACCOUNTS' ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
          IF bseg-hkont BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
            CLEAR flg_nohkont.
            EXIT.
          ELSE.
            flg_nohkont = 'X'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDSELECT.

* For accounts common to Cash and Bank (91299) no check reqd
        SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE  setname  = 'CASH_ACCOUNTS'.
          IF bseg-hkont BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
            flg_nohkont = 'X'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDSELECT.

        IF flg_nohkont = 'X'.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        IF bseg-shkzg = 'H'.           " Credit
          sum_credit = sum_credit + bseg-wrbtr.
        ELSEIF bseg-shkzg = 'S'.       "Debit.
          sum_debit  = sum_debit  + bseg-wrbtr.
        ENDIF.

      ENDLOOP.

      IF bkpf-blart = 'BP' AND ( sum_credit < sum_debit ).
        b_result = b_false.
      ENDIF.

      IF bkpf-blart = 'BR' AND ( sum_credit > sum_debit ).
        b_result = b_false.
      ENDIF.

* added on 150401 to prevent CP CR postings without cash accounts

      IF sum_credit = 0 AND sum_debit = 0.
        b_result = b_false.
      ENDIF.
  ENDCASE.
**  code begin by AD  '' 24.01.2023.
*  IF sy-tcode = 'FBR2' OR sy-tcode = 'FB1D'.
*    IF bkpf-blart = 'CB' OR    bkpf-blart = 'OS'.
*      MESSAGE 'Do not clear document with document type' TYPE 'E'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
* ended by AD '' 24.01.2023
ENDFORM.                                                    "uf103

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------
*&      Form  GET_SETNAME
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*      -->P_ACCOUNT  text
*      <--P_SETNAME  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM get_setname USING    p_account
                 CHANGING p_setname.

  DATA : flg_setfound.

  CLEAR : p_setname,
          flg_setfound.

* Bank Clearing Account
  SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE setname = 'BANK_CLG_ACCOUNTS' ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.

    IF p_account BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
      p_setname = setleaf-setname.
      EXIT.
      flg_setfound = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDSELECT.

  CHECK flg_setfound IS INITIAL.
* Bank Main Account
  SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE setname = 'BANK_MAIN_ACCOUNTS' ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
    IF p_account BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
      p_setname = setleaf-setname.
      EXIT.
      flg_setfound = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDSELECT.

  CHECK flg_setfound IS INITIAL.
* Cash Account
  SELECT * FROM  setleaf WHERE setname = 'CASH_ACCOUNTS' ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
    IF p_account BETWEEN setleaf-valfrom AND setleaf-valto.
      p_setname = setleaf-setname.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDSELECT.
ENDFORM.                               " GET_SETNAME

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------
*
*&      Form  FISYEARRANGE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM fisyearrange.

  CALL FUNCTION 'GET_DATES_FROM_GJAHR'
    EXPORTING
      ip_fikrs       = 'ONGC'
      ip_gjahr       = w_gjahr
    IMPORTING
      op_datab       = w_llimit
      op_datbis      = w_hlimit
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_occurred = 1
      OTHERS         = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    WRITE:' Date not obtained'.
  ENDIF.
  w_duedate-low = w_llimit.
  w_duedate-high = w_hlimit.
  w_duedate-sign = 'I'.
  w_duedate-option = 'BT'.
  APPEND w_duedate.

ENDFORM.                    " FISYEARRANGE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------
*&      Form  GETFISCALYEAR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM getfiscalyear.

  CALL FUNCTION 'FM_GET_YEAR_FROM_DATE'
    EXPORTING
      i_applc             = 'A'
      i_farea             = 'ONGC'
      i_date              = p_budat
    IMPORTING
      e_year              = w_gjahr
    EXCEPTIONS
      applc_not_supported = 1
      no_periv            = 2
      OTHERS              = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " GETFISCALYEAR

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM U128                                                      *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule                          *
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.        *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False                                *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM u128 USING b_result.
  DATA : l_agr_name TYPE agr_users-agr_name,
         l_sy_subrc TYPE sy-subrc.                "FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.8

* According to revised FS-FI-AA-004 dated 15/09/2003 ,Validation is to
* perform on table USR02 and ZFI_INDENTOR. If Indentor code doesnot
* exist in table USR02, then Check Indentor code further in table
* ZFI_INDENTOR. If the value does not exist in Z TABLE or Table USR02
* then the system should give an error message

* Commented on 27/10/2004 as per revised FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.7
*  SELECT SINGLE * FROM  ZUSRMST WHERE  CPFNO = ANLZ-KFZKZ AND
*                               COMPANY_CODE  = ANLA-BUKRS.

* Added on 27/10/2004 as per revised FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.7
*  SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zusrmst WHERE  cpfno = anlz-kfzkz. "Commented
  "on 05/12/2005 as per revised FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.8

*  if zusrmst-del_flag = 'X' .
*    message e241(zfi) .
*  endif .

* Start - As per revised FS FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.8, validation of user is
* to perform on table USR02 and ZFI_INDENTOR. If kfzkz is available in
* usr02 then it should check that user has either of the following role
* attached with his ID
*                    -  FI_ASSET_INDENTER_XXX_YY
*                    -  MM_ASSET_INDENTER_XXX_YY
*                    -  MM_TEMPORARY_INDENTER_XXX_YY

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM  usr02 WHERE  bname = anlz-kfzkz.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    CLEAR : l_agr_name.

    CONCATENATE text-050 text-052 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name.

    PERFORM chk_role_indentor USING    l_agr_name anlz-kfzkz
                              CHANGING l_sy_subrc.

    IF l_sy_subrc = 4.
      MESSAGE w037(zaa).
    ENDIF.

  ELSE.

* End   - FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.8

* If Indentor code doesnot exist in table USR02, check same indentor
* code in table ZFI_INDENTOR
*  IF sy-subrc <> 0. "Commented on 05/12/2005 as per revised
    "FS-FI-AA-004 Ver 1.8


    SELECT SINGLE kfzkz FROM zfi_indentor INTO (zfi_indentor-kfzkz)
                                        WHERE  bukrs = anla-bukrs AND
                                                 kfzkz = anlz-kfzkz.

*If indentor code doesnt exist in ZFI_INDENTOR then display errors
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE e039(zfi).
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                                                    "u128

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  chk_role_indentor
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*   To check receiving indentor is having FI_ASSET_INDENTOR_XXX_YY     *
*   role attached with his ID or not ( where XXX is company code in    *
*   which transaction is being made)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->L_AGR_NAME              Role Name
*      -->L_RECV_IND              Indentor
*      <--L_SY_SUBRC              Return Value
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM chk_role_indentor USING    l_agr_name
                                l_recv_ind
                       CHANGING l_sy_subrc.

  DATA  : l_agr_name_t TYPE agr_users-agr_name.

  CLEAR : l_sy_subrc.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users
                WHERE uname     = l_recv_ind
                   AND agr_name = l_agr_name.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Further validation on Indentor Code
* Checks any of these Roles attched with Indentor  -
*                MM_ASSET_INDENTER_XXX_YY or
*                MM_TEMPORARY_INDENTER_XXX_YY
* if not , display a warning message
    CONCATENATE text-051 text-052 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name_t.
    "MM_ASSET_INDENTER_XXX_YY

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users
                  WHERE uname     = l_recv_ind
                     AND agr_name = l_agr_name_t.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

      CLEAR l_agr_name_t.

      CONCATENATE text-051 text-053 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name_t.
      "MM_TEMPORARY_INDENTER_XXX_YY

      SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users
                    WHERE uname     = l_recv_ind
                       AND agr_name = l_agr_name_t.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        l_sy_subrc = 4.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : agr_users.

ENDFORM.                    " chk_role_indentor

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM u132                                                    *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Validation of cash posting date based on date stored in zfi_gl_denom
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  B_RESULT                                                      *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------
FORM u132 USING b_result.

" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*  SELECT SINGLE hkont MAX( budat ) INTO (zfi_gl_denom-hkont,
*                                         zfi_gl_denom-budat)
*                            FROM zfi_gl_denom WHERE hkont = bseg-hkont
*                            GROUP BY hkont.
 SELECT HKONT, MAX( BUDAT )   "#EC CI_NOORDER
   FROM ZFI_GL_DENOM
    UP TO 1 ROWS
   INTO (@ZFI_GL_DENOM-HKONT , @ZFI_GL_DENOM-BUDAT)
   WHERE HKONT = @BSEG-HKONT
GROUP BY HKONT .
 ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC

  IF bkpf-budat <= zfi_gl_denom-budat.
    MESSAGE e463(zfi) WITH zfi_gl_denom-budat bseg-hkont bseg-bukrs.
  ENDIF.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM u133                                                   *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*Validation of cash posting date based on date stored in zfi_gl_denom
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  B_RESULT                                                      *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------
FORM u138 USING b_result.

  DATA : l_out(20) .
  DATA : l_year     LIKE vtvdeta_tr-dgjahr,
         l_yearbseg LIKE vtvdeta_tr-dgjahr.

  CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
    EXPORTING
      input  = bseg-kblnr
    IMPORTING
      output = l_out.

  SELECT SINGLE fdatk INTO (kblp-fdatk) FROM kblp WHERE
                                        belnr = l_out  AND
                                        blpos = bseg-kblpos .

  CALL FUNCTION 'FTI_FISCAL_YEAR_MONTH_GET'
    EXPORTING
      i_bukrs = bkpf-bukrs
      i_budat = kblp-fdatk
    IMPORTING
      e_gjahr = l_year.

  CALL FUNCTION 'FTI_FISCAL_YEAR_MONTH_GET'
    EXPORTING
      i_bukrs = bkpf-bukrs
      i_budat = bkpf-budat
    IMPORTING
      e_gjahr = l_yearbseg.

  IF l_yearbseg < l_year .
    MESSAGE  e099(zfi) WITH l_out bseg-kblpos kblp-fdatk.

  ENDIF .

ENDFORM.                                                    "u138

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM u135                                                    *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Validation of cash posting date based on date stored ZFI_DTYPE_GLCODE
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  B_RESULT                                                      *
*--------------------------------------------------------------------
FORM u135 USING b_result.

  DATA : l_msg       TYPE string,
         all_posting LIKE zfi_dtype_glcode-allowed_posting,
         al_posting  LIKE zfi_dtype_glcode-allowed_posting,
         N(7)        TYPE n,
         s_flag      TYPE c,
         l_indi      TYPE c.

  SELECT SINGLE allowed_posting INTO (al_posting ) FROM zfi_dtype_glcode
                                          WHERE gl_acc = bseg-hkont  AND
                                               do_type = bkpf-blart  AND
                                                   co_code = bkpf-bukrs.

  IF al_posting = 'X' OR al_posting = 'Y' OR al_posting = ' ' OR
                         al_posting = 'H' OR al_posting = 'S' .
    IF l_count IS INITIAL .
      ist_final2-newbk = rf05a-newbk .
      ist_final2-hkont = bseg-hkont.
      ist_final2-blart = bkpf-blart.
      ist_final2-wrbtr = bseg-wrbtr.
      ist_final2-alp = al_posting.
      n_flag = 'Z'.
      APPEND ist_final2.
      l_indi = 'X' . l_count = l_count + 1 .
      PERFORM calculate_amt .
    ELSE .
      READ TABLE ist_final2 WITH KEY newbk = rf05a-newbk
                                     hkont = bseg-hkont .
      IF sy-subrc NE 0 .
        ist_final2-newbk = rf05a-newbk .
        ist_final2-hkont = bseg-hkont.
        ist_final2-blart = bkpf-blart.
        ist_final2-wrbtr = bseg-wrbtr.
        ist_final2-alp = al_posting.
        n_flag = 'Z'.
        APPEND ist_final2.
        PERFORM calculate_amt .
      ELSE .
        PERFORM calculate_amt .
      ENDIF .

    ENDIF .
  ENDIF .

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_dtype_glcode WHERE gl_acc = bseg-hkont AND
                                             do_type = bkpf-blart AND
                                             co_code = bkpf-bukrs.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SELECT SINGLE  shkzg INTO (dc_ind)  FROM tbsl WHERE
                                               bschl = bseg-bschl.
    SELECT SINGLE allowed_posting INTO (all_posting)
                                     FROM zfi_dtype_glcode
                                    WHERE gl_acc = bseg-hkont AND
                                         do_type = bkpf-blart AND
                                         co_code = bkpf-bukrs.
    IF all_posting = 'H' OR all_posting = 'S'.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_dtype_glcode WHERE gl_acc = bseg-hkont AND
                                                 do_type = bkpf-blart AND
                                                 co_code = bkpf-bukrs AND
                                                allowed_posting = dc_ind.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        IF dc_ind = 'H'.
          l_msg = 'DEBIT'.
        ELSEIF dc_ind = 'S'.
          l_msg = 'CREDIT'.
        ENDIF.
        MESSAGE e090(zfi) WITH bkpf-blart bseg-hkont
        l_msg.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                                                    "u135
*---------------------------------------------------------------------
*       FORM u136                                                    *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------
* Validation of cash posting date based on date stored in
* ZFI_DTYPE_GLCODE based on document complete .
*---------------------------------------------------------------------
*  -->  B_RESULT
*---------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM u136 USING b_result.

  DATA:  l_msg  TYPE string,
         l_flag TYPE c,
         l_exit TYPE c.
  IF NOT ( sy-tcode = 'FB09' OR sy-tcode = 'FB02' OR sy-tcode = 'FBL1N' ).
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_dtype_glcode WHERE
                           do_type = bkpf-blart AND
                           co_code = bkpf-bukrs .
    IF sy-subrc = 0 .
      LOOP AT ist_final2.
        SELECT * FROM zfi_dtype_glcode WHERE gl_acc = ist_final2-hkont
                                        AND do_type = ist_final2-blart
                                        AND co_code = bkpf-bukrs.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            l_flag = 'X'.
            l_exit = 'X' .
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDSELECT.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF l_flag <> 'X'.
        MESSAGE e465(zfi)  WITH ist_final2-blart ist_final2-newbk.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF .
    IF NOT l_exit IS INITIAL .
      LOOP AT ist_final2.
        IF ist_final2-alp = 'X'.
          IF ist_final2-wrbtr < '0.01'.
            l_msg = ' Net DEBIT'.
            MESSAGE e090(zfi) WITH ist_final2-blart ist_final2-hkont
                                   l_msg ist_final2-wrbtr.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF ist_final2-alp = 'Y'.
          IF ist_final2-wrbtr > '0.01'.
            l_msg = ' Net CREDIT'.
            MESSAGE e090(zfi) WITH ist_final2-blart ist_final2-hkont
                                  l_msg ist_final2-wrbtr.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF .
  ENDIF .

ENDFORM.                                                    "u136

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM calculate_amt                                            *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ........                                                      *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM calculate_amt .

  DATA :  l_indi TYPE c .

  l_count  = l_count + 1 .
  IF ist_final2-hkont = bseg-hkont .
    SELECT SINGLE  shkzg INTO (dc_ind)  FROM tbsl WHERE
                                     bschl = bseg-bschl.
    IF dc_ind = 'H'.
      IF sy-tabix = 1  AND n_flag = 'Z'.
        ist_final2-wrbtr = 0 - bseg-wrbtr .
        n_flag = 'X'.
      ELSE .
        ist_final2-wrbtr = ist_final2-wrbtr - bseg-wrbtr.
      ENDIF .
      MODIFY  ist_final2 INDEX sy-tabix . .
      l_indi = 'X' . l_ind1 = 'X' .
    ELSEIF dc_ind = 'S' .
      IF sy-tabix = 1 AND l_ind1 = ' '  AND n_flag = 'Z'.
        ist_final2-wrbtr = 0 + bseg-wrbtr .
        n_flag = 'X'.
      ELSE .
        ist_final2-wrbtr = ist_final2-wrbtr + bseg-wrbtr.
      ENDIF .
      MODIFY ist_final2 INDEX sy-tabix .
      l_indi = 'X' .
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "calculate_amt

*      Validate WorkFlow data maintenance. At the time of FI document

*      maintained in any of these tables do not allow document to be
*      PARKED.
*---------------------------------------------------------------------

FORM u137 USING b_result.

  TABLES : t001, zfi_fipp_wftype .
  DATA : sysubrc    LIKE sy-subrc,
         l_xfrge(1) .
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*  SELECT SINGLE * FROM vbkpf WHERE belnr = bkpf-belnr AND
*                                   bukrs = bkpf-bukrs AND
*                                   gjahr = bkpf-gjahr .
 SELECT * FROM VBKPF   UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE BELNR = BKPF-BELNR AND BUKRS =
 BKPF-BUKRS AND GJAHR = BKPF-GJAHR   ORDER BY  AUSBK BUKRS BELNR GJAHR .
   ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
  IF sy-subrc = 0 .
    l_xfrge = vbkpf-xfrge .
  ELSE .
    l_xfrge = 'X' .
  ENDIF   .
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM t001 WHERE bukrs = bkpf-bukrs .
  IF NOT l_xfrge IS INITIAL .
    IF NOT t001-wfvar IS INITIAL .
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_fipp_wftype WHERE blart = bkpf-blart .
      IF sy-subrc = 0 .

                                 bseg-hkont

**---------------------------------------------------------------------
**       FORM u140                                                  *
**---------------------------------------------------------------------
**   Validation for cheking Transfer In Equal to Transfer Out Per eact
**set maintained in a superset
** Collecting the amount entered in each line item with Glaccount
** S.R.Sudha 7.4.2005  Change id :+001  FS-FI-GL-027
**---------------------------------------------------------------------
FORM u140 USING b_result.

  SELECT * APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_set  FROM
                setleaf WHERE setname = 'ZERO_SUM_ACCOUNTS_SET'.

  LOOP AT ist_set.
    SELECT *  APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_acct FROM setleaf
                                          WHERE setname = ist_set-valfrom .
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT ist_acct BY setname.

  IF bkpf-blart = 'IU'.
    LOOP AT ist_acct .
      ist_acct1 = ist_acct.
      r_acct-sign = ist_acct-valsign.
      r_acct-option = ist_acct-valoption.
      r_acct-low = ist_acct-valfrom.
      r_acct-high = ist_acct-valto.
      APPEND r_acct.
      AT END OF setname.

        IF bseg-hkont IN r_acct.
          CLEAR wa_col.
          READ TABLE ist_col INTO wa_col WITH  KEY
                                   setname = ist_acct1-setname
                                   hkont = bseg-hkont
                                   buzei = bseg-buzei .
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
            wa_col-setname = ist_acct1-setname.
            wa_col-hkont = bseg-hkont.
            wa_col-buzei = bseg-buzei.

            SELECT SINGLE  shkzg INTO  (cd_ind)  FROM tbsl WHERE
             bschl = bseg-bschl.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              IF cd_ind = 'H'.
                l_wrbtr = 0 - bseg-wrbtr .
              ELSEIF cd_ind = 'S'.
                l_wrbtr = 0 + bseg-wrbtr.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
            wa_col-wrbtr = l_wrbtr.
            CLEAR l_wrbtr.
            APPEND wa_col TO ist_col.
          ELSEIF sy-subrc = 0.
            SELECT SINGLE  shkzg INTO  (cd_ind)  FROM tbsl WHERE
             bschl = bseg-bschl.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              IF cd_ind = 'H'.
                l_wrbtr = 0 - bseg-wrbtr .
              ELSEIF cd_ind = 'S'.
                l_wrbtr = 0 + bseg-wrbtr.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
            wa_col-wrbtr  = l_wrbtr.
            CLEAR l_wrbtr.
            MODIFY  ist_col FROM wa_col INDEX sy-tabix TRANSPORTING wrbtr
                                                                        .
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.  " BSE-HKONT IN
        CLEAR r_acct.
        REFRESH r_acct.
      ENDAT.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.
  CLEAR ist_acct.
  REFRESH ist_acct.

ENDFORM.                                                    "u140

**---------------------------------------------------------------------
**       FORM u141                                                    *
**---------------------------------------------------------------------
**   Validation for checking Transfer In Equal to Transfer Out Per eact
**set maintained in a superset
**The collected amount is checked here setwise whether setwise total is
**zero . If it is not then error message is displayed accordingly
** S.R.Sudha  7.4.2005   As per FS-FI-Gl-027
**---------------------------------------------------------------------
*
FORM u141
   CHANGING b_result.



  SORT ist_col BY setname hkont.
  LOOP AT ist_col INTO wa_col.
    wa_col1 = wa_col.
    AT END OF setname .
      SUM.
      l_wrbtr  = wa_col-wrbtr.
      wa_fin-setname  = wa_col1-setname.
      wa_fin-wrbtr = l_wrbtr.
      APPEND wa_fin TO ist_fin.
      CLEAR l_wrbtr.
      CLEAR wa_fin.
    ENDAT.
  ENDLOOP.


  LOOP AT ist_fin INTO wa_fin.
    IF wa_fin-wrbtr = '0.00'.
      DELETE ist_fin.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF NOT ist_fin  IS INITIAL.
    LOOP AT ist_fin INTO wa_fin.
      CASE sy-tabix.
        WHEN '1'.
          wa_disp-set1 =  wa_fin-setname.
        WHEN '2'.
          wa_disp-set2 = wa_fin-setname.
        WHEN '3'.
          wa_disp-set3 =  wa_fin-setname.
        WHEN '4'.
          wa_disp-set4 =   wa_fin-setname .
        WHEN '5'.
          wa_disp-set5 =   wa_fin-setname .
        WHEN '6'.
          wa_disp-set6 =   wa_fin-setname .
      ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
    CLEAR ist_fin.
    REFRESH ist_fin.

    MESSAGE e256(zfi) WITH wa_disp.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR ist_col.
  REFRESH ist_col.
  CLEAR wa_disp.
  CLEAR ist_fin.
  REFRESH ist_fin.


ENDFORM.                                                    "u141
*---------------------------------------------------------------------
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  chk_glcode
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_BSEG_BUKRS  text
*      -->P_BSEG_HKONT  text
*      <--P_SYSUBRC  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM chk_glcode USING    l_bukrs
                         l_hkont
                CHANGING l_sysubrc.
  DATA : l_lines TYPE i .

  TABLES : zfi_fipp_cashier .
  DESCRIBE TABLE ist_skb1 LINES l_lines.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM  setleaf
  WHERE  setname  = 'WF_CASHIER_NOT_REQUIRED'
  AND valfrom LE l_hkont
  AND valto   GE l_hkont.

  IF sy-subrc NE 0.

" " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
    SELECT SINGLE *                      "#EC CI_DB_OPERATION_OK[2431747]
      FROM ska1 WHERE ktopl = 'ONGC' AND "#EC CI_DB_OPERATION_OK[2389136]
                                    saknr = l_hkont AND
                                    ktoks IN ('CBNK', 'CJVA') .
" " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
    IF sy-subrc = 0 .
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_fipp_cashier WHERE saknr = l_hkont AND
                                                     xloek <> 'X' AND
                                                     bukrs = l_bukrs .
      IF sy-subrc <> 0 .
        MESSAGE e074(zfi) WITH l_hkont .
      ENDIF .
      IF l_lines = 0 .
        MESSAGE w096(zfi).
      ENDIF .
    ELSE .
" " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
      SELECT  * APPENDING TABLE ist_skb1 FROM skb1 WHERE "#EC CI_DB_OPERATION_OK[2431747]
                                       bukrs = l_bukrs AND
                                       saknr = l_hkont AND
                                       busab <> ' ' .
" " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
    ENDIF .
  ELSE.
" " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
    SELECT  * APPENDING TABLE ist_skb1 FROM skb1 WHERE "#EC CI_DB_OPERATION_OK[2431747]
                                     bukrs = l_bukrs AND
                                     saknr = l_hkont AND
                                     busab <> ' ' .
" " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
  ENDIF.
  l_sysubrc = '1' .
ENDFORM.                    " chk_glcode

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  chk_rules
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_BKPF_USNAM  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM chk_rules USING    p_bkpf_usnam.
  DATA : ist_rules TYPE TABLE OF zfi_fipp_rules WITH HEADER LINE .
  DATA : l_lines TYPE i .
  TABLES : zfi_fipp_rules .
  SELECT * INTO TABLE ist_rules FROM zfi_fipp_rules WHERE
                             busab_low LE ist_skb1-busab AND
                             busab_high GE ist_skb1-busab .

  DESCRIBE TABLE ist_rules LINES l_lines.
  IF l_lines = 0 .
    MESSAGE e218(zfi).
  ELSE .
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zfi_fipp_rules
                                 WHERE ( account_asst   = p_bkpf_usnam
                                 OR      supervisor_e0  = p_bkpf_usnam
                                 OR      supervisor_e1  = p_bkpf_usnam
                                 OR      supervisor_e2  = p_bkpf_usnam
                                 OR      supervisor_e3  = p_bkpf_usnam
                                 OR      supervisor_e4  = p_bkpf_usnam )
                                 AND    xloek  = space.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 .
      MESSAGE e219(zfi) .
    ENDIF .
  ENDIF .
ENDFORM.                    " chk_rules

                                              bukrs  = bseg-bukrs .

**---------------------------------------------------------------------*
**       FORM ufg2                                                     *
**---------------------------------------------------------------------*
**                                                                     *
**   FS-FI-GL-023                                                      *
**---------------------------------------------------------------------*
** User Exit to stop posting before BAPC Posting Key date              *
** Users should not allow FI Posting before key date of BAPC Run to    *
** maintain that trial balance is tallied per business area per month. *
**---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ufg2 USING b_result.

  DATA l_sttag LIKE rf180-sttag.
  DATA l_aftlf LIKE rf180-aftlf.

  SELECT MAX( aftlf ) INTO l_aftlf FROM rf180 WHERE lstrn = ' '
                                            AND   bukrs = bseg-bukrs
*                                            and   sttag > bkpf-budat.
                                            AND   sttag >= bkpf-budat.

  IF l_aftlf > 0.
    SELECT SINGLE sttag  INTO l_sttag FROM rf180 WHERE lstrn = ' '
                                              AND   bukrs = bseg-bukrs
*                                              and   sttag > bkpf-budat
                                              AND   sttag >= bkpf-budat
                                              AND   aftlf = l_aftlf.

    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      MESSAGE e482(zfi) WITH l_sttag.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "ufg2

**----------------------------------------------------------------------
**
**       FORM UF203
**       AUTHOR: SAB_KBHAT  Changed By CAB_SUDHA  20.2.2006
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
**
**       Example of an exit for a boolean rule
**
**       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.
**
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
**
**  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False
**
**----------------------------------------------------------------------
**
FORM uf203 USING b_result.
  DATA l_lines TYPE i.

*Fetch data from setleaf for set business transaction,docutment type
*and account number
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM setleaf INTO gt_setleaf
            WHERE setclass = g_setclass
              AND  setname  = g_setname2
              AND  valfrom  = bseg-hkont.

*  READ TABLE   Gt_setleaf  WITH KEY  setclass = g_setclass
*                                    setname  = g_setname2
*                                    valfrom  = bseg-hkont.

  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    APPEND  jvap  TO gt_jvap_user.

    SORT gt_jvap_user BY rjvnam.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_jvap_user COMPARING rjvnam.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_jvap_user LINES l_lines.

    IF l_lines > 1.

      MESSAGE e029(zjva).

    ENDIF.

  ELSE.

    APPEND jvap TO gt_jvap_noset.

  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_jvap_noset.

    IF NOT gt_jvap_user[] IS INITIAL.

      READ TABLE gt_jvap_user WITH KEY rjvnam = gt_jvap_noset-rjvnam.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.

        MESSAGE e029(zjva).

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                                                    "uf203
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM UF204
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule                          *
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.        *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False                                *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM uf204 USING b_result.

  DATA: ist_zkaps LIKE STANDARD TABLE OF zkaps,
        wa_zkaps  TYPE zkaps,
        l_string  TYPE string,
        wa_tj01t  TYPE tj01t.


  DATA : l_kokrs TYPE bkpf-fikrs,
         l_bukrs TYPE bkpf-bukrs,
         l_gjahr TYPE bkpf-gjahr,
         l_monat TYPE bkpf-monat,
         l_vrgng TYPE cobk-vrgng,
         l_versn TYPE cobk-versn,
         l_txt   TYPE tj01t-txt,
         l_vrg   TYPE string.

  DATA: l_text TYPE string.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs> TYPE any.
  DATA: c1(13) VALUE 'WA_ZKAPS-PSP0'.

  l_kokrs = cobk-kokrs.
  l_bukrs = cobl-bukrs.
  l_gjahr = cobk-gjahr.
  l_monat = cobk-perab.
  l_vrgng = cobk-vrgng.

*Select txt from tj01 for vrgng
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM tj01t INTO wa_tj01t
              WHERE vrgng = l_vrgng
              AND   spras = sy-langu.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    l_txt = wa_tj01t-txt.
  ENDIF.

  CONCATENATE '(' l_txt ')' INTO l_vrg.
*Select record from zkaps
  SELECT * FROM zkaps INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_zkaps
      WHERE
                kokrs = l_kokrs AND
                vrgng = l_vrgng AND
                gjahr = l_gjahr AND
                refbk = l_bukrs.

*Code for printing message for period locking
  IF l_monat > 0.     "Kalpesh 21.12.2005

    LOOP AT ist_zkaps INTO wa_zkaps.

      CONCATENATE c1 l_monat INTO l_string.

      ASSIGN (l_string) TO <fs>.
      IF <fs> = 'X'.
        MESSAGE e412(zco) WITH l_bukrs l_vrgng l_vrg.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "uf204

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM U142                                                    *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Example of an exit for a boolean rule                          *
*       This exit can be used in FI for callup points 1,2 or 3.        *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False                                *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM u142 USING b_result.
  DATA : l_ktopl TYPE t001-ktopl,
         l_korrk TYPE t030u-korrk.

  SELECT SINGLE ktopl INTO l_ktopl FROM t001
                         WHERE bukrs = bseg-bukrs.

  SELECT SINGLE korrk INTO l_korrk FROM t030u
         WHERE ktopl = l_ktopl
           AND ktosl IN ('GA1','GA2','GA3','GA4','GA5')
           AND korrk = bseg-hkont.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    b_result = b_false.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "u142
*   Start of Modification by SAB_MURTHY on 9th September, 2013.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  U143
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->B_RESULT   text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM u143 USING b_result.
  DATA : lv_hkont TYPE hkont,
         lv_msg   TYPE bapi_msg.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_gl_movement WHERE hkont EQ bseg-hkont.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    CLEAR: lv_hkont,lv_msg,zfi_gl_movement.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_gl_movement
                 WHERE hkont EQ bseg-hkont
                  AND  bewar EQ bseg-bewar.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CONCATENATE 'Enter Correct Transaction Type Relevant for G/L Account :' bseg-hkont
      INTO lv_msg.
      MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'E'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR: lv_hkont,lv_msg.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                      "u142
*   End of Modification by SAB_MURTHY on 9th September, 2013.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM U147                                                      *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* FS NO. : FS-FI-OBV-001
* On executing trans.code AS02, it allow to change the value for field
* ANLA-ORD42.It is required that value for this field should only be
* changed by Indenter & verification team through BDC which already exit
* for updating STRV & verification status.
*
* This will not be applicable if ANLA-BUKRS = OBV,OVL,JVB,JVC,JVJ,JVM,
* JVP,JVR,JVD,JVK
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  <--  B_RESULT    T = True  F = False                                *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM u147 USING b_result.
  DATA : l_agr_name TYPE agr_users-agr_name,
         l_sy_subrc TYPE sy-subrc.

  DATA :l_ord42 TYPE anla-ord42.

  IF sy-tcode = 'AS02'.
    IF anla-bukrs = 'OBV' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'OVL' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVB' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVC' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVJ' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVM' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVP' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVR' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVD' OR
       anla-bukrs = 'JVK'.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : l_sy_subrc.

      CONCATENATE text-050 text-052 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name.

      PERFORM chk_role_indentor_verifier USING    l_agr_name
                                                  sy-uname
                                         CHANGING l_sy_subrc.

      IF l_sy_subrc <> 0.

        CONCATENATE text-050 text-129 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name.

        PERFORM chk_role_indentor_verifier USING    l_agr_name
                                                    sy-uname
                                           CHANGING l_sy_subrc.

        IF l_sy_subrc <> 0.

*+SP001 - Start
          CONCATENATE text-051 text-052 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name.
          "MM_ASSET_INDENTER_XXX_YY

          PERFORM chk_role_indentor_verifier USING    l_agr_name
                                                      sy-uname
                                             CHANGING l_sy_subrc.

          IF l_sy_subrc <> 0.

            CONCATENATE text-051 text-053 anla-bukrs text-054 INTO l_agr_name.
            "MM_TEMPORARY_INDENTER_XXX_YY

            PERFORM chk_role_indentor_verifier USING    l_agr_name
                                                        sy-uname
                                               CHANGING l_sy_subrc.

            IF l_sy_subrc <> 0.

*+SP001- End

              CLEAR : l_ord42.

              SELECT SINGLE ord42 FROM anla INTO l_ord42
                        WHERE bukrs = anla-bukrs AND
                              anln1 = anla-anln1 AND
                              anln2 = anla-anln2.

              IF l_ord42 <> anla-ord42.
                MOVE l_ord42 TO anla-ord42.
                MESSAGE e067(zaa).
              ENDIF.

            ENDIF.                                          "+SP001
          ENDIF.                                            "+SP001
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                                                    "u147

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  chk_role_indentor_verifier
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*   To check Indentor/verifer is having FI_ASSET_INDENTER_XXX_YY or
*   FI_ASSET_VERIFY_XXX_YY role attached with his ID or not ( where XXX
*   is company code in which transaction is being made)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_AGR_NAME              Role Name
*      -->P_UNAME                 Indentor / Verifier
*      <--P_SY_SUBRC              Return Value
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM chk_role_indentor_verifier USING    p_agr_name
                                         p_uname
                                CHANGING p_sy_subrc.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users
                WHERE uname     = p_uname
                   AND agr_name = p_agr_name.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    p_sy_subrc = 4.
  ELSE.
    p_sy_subrc = 0.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " chk_role_indentor_verifier

FORM uf205 USING bool_data TYPE gb002_015
CHANGING b_result.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  DATA: l_hcount  TYPE i.
  DATA : l_scount TYPE i.
*DATA : "ist_doc  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_doc ,
*       ist_doc1  TYPE TABLE OF ty_doc .
*       wa_doc  TYPE ty_doc.

*As per request from Mr.Parag Travel Mangement Consultant on 15.05.2008
* IF bkpf-blart <> 'TM'.

  CLEAR ist_doc.
  REFRESH ist_doc.
  CLEAR wa_doc.
  CLEAR l_scount.
  CLEAR l_hcount.

  IF NOT bool_data IS INITIAL.
    CHECK NOT bool_data-bseg IS INITIAL.
    LOOP AT bool_data-bseg INTO bseg.
      wa_doc-buzei = bseg-buzei.
      wa_doc-shkzg = bseg-shkzg.
      wa_doc-augbl = bseg-augbl.
      wa_doc-koart = bseg-koart.
      wa_doc-bschl = bseg-bschl .
      wa_doc-hkont = bseg-hkont.
      wa_doc-lifnr = bseg-lifnr.
      wa_doc-rebzg = bseg-rebzg.
      wa_doc-xauto = bseg-xauto.
      APPEND wa_doc TO ist_doc.
      APPEND wa_doc TO ist_doc1.
      CLEAR wa_doc.
    ENDLOOP.
    ist_doc[] = ist_doc1[] .
    DELETE ist_doc WHERE koart <> 'K'.
    DELETE ist_doc1 WHERE koart <> 'K'.
*---ICC exculsion
    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000120107'.
    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000190591'.
    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000190592'.
    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000190593'.

    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000100701'.
    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000100702'.
    DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000100703'.




*    DELETE  ist_doc WHERE hkont = '0000100701' or '0000100702' or
*'0000100703' .
*-----employees
    DELETE ist_doc1 WHERE  lifnr+0(5) = '00170'.
*-----+006
*    delete ist_doc where  lifnr+0(5) = '00070'.
* CPF no rangesstarted    from  71-79.....
    DELETE ist_doc1 WHERE  lifnr+0(4) = '0007'.

*-----+006
*---deposits
*DELETE  ist_doc WHERE hkont = '0000190591' or '0000190592' or
*'0000190593' .


*--partial clearing
    DELETE ist_doc1 WHERE ( NOT rebzg IS INITIAL AND xauto = 'X' ).

*-------
    IF NOT ist_doc1 IS INITIAL.
      DATA : ist_doc    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_doc,
             wa_ist_doc TYPE  ty_doc.

**AMIT STARTS  CR 30007343
      DATA : ist_setleaf1 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf,
             ist_setleaf2 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf,
             ist1_lfa1    TYPE TABLE OF lfa1,
             ist1_lfa2    TYPE TABLE OF lfa1.

      DATA : wa_ist_setleaf1 TYPE  setleaf,
             wa_ist1_lfa1    TYPE  lfa1.
      SELECT  * FROM setleaf    " Now Users maintained in set 'USERS_IUTEXEMPT' will be exempted from the IUT posting validation.
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf1
      WHERE setclass = '0000'
      AND setname = 'VENDOR_SPLGLZERO'
      AND valoption = 'EQ'.


      LOOP AT ist_doc1 INTO wa_ist_doc.
        SELECT * FROM lfa1    " Now Users maintained in set 'USERS_IUTEXEMPT' will be exempted from the IUT posting validation.
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist1_lfa1
        WHERE  lifnr = wa_ist_doc-lifnr.

      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT ist1_lfa1 INTO wa_ist1_lfa1.
        SELECT  * FROM setleaf    " Now Users maintained in set 'USERS_IUTEXEMPT' will be exempted from the IUT posting validation.
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf2
        WHERE
        setname = 'VENDORGROUP_SPLGLZERO'
        AND valoption = 'EQ' AND   valfrom =  wa_ist1_lfa1-ktokk.
      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT ist_setleaf2 INTO wa_ist_setleaf1.
        SELECT * FROM lfa1    " Now Users maintained in set 'USERS_IUTEXEMPT' will be exempted from the IUT posting validation.
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist1_lfa2
        WHERE ktokk = wa_ist_setleaf1-valfrom .

      ENDLOOP.



      LOOP AT ist_setleaf1 INTO wa_ist_setleaf1.
        DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000101199' AND  lifnr = wa_ist_setleaf1-valfrom.
      ENDLOOP.
      LOOP AT ist1_lfa2 INTO wa_ist1_lfa1.

        DELETE  ist_doc1 WHERE hkont = '0000101199' AND lifnr = wa_ist1_lfa1-lifnr.
      ENDLOOP.

*AMIT ENDS CR 30007343
*----exclude 21&39 if both exist
      READ TABLE ist_doc1 INTO wa_doc WITH KEY
      bschl = '21' .
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        READ TABLE ist_doc1 INTO wa_doc WITH KEY
        bschl = '39' .
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE ist_doc1 INTO wa_doc WITH KEY
      bschl = '39' .
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        READ TABLE ist_doc1 INTO wa_doc WITH KEY
        bschl = '21' .
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      LOOP AT  ist_doc1 INTO wa_doc.
        IF wa_doc-shkzg = 'H'.
          l_hcount  = l_hcount + 1 .
        ELSEIF wa_doc-shkzg = 'S'.
          l_scount = l_scount + 1.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

*---check whether one entry is from  clearing
      IF   l_hcount  = 0 AND l_scount = 0.
      ELSE.
        IF l_hcount = 0 AND l_scount > 0.
        ELSEIF l_scount = 0 AND l_hcount > 0.
        ELSE.
          READ TABLE ist_doc1 INTO wa_doc  WITH KEY augbl = '*'.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
          ELSE.
            MESSAGE  e311(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "uf205

  DATA : w_dmbtr     TYPE bseg-dmbtr,
         w_ebeln     TYPE bseg-ebeln,
         w_ebelp     TYPE bseg-ebelp,
         w_netwr     TYPE ekpo-netwr,

         wbl_qty     TYPE ekpo-menge,
         wbl_value   TYPE bseg-dmbtr,
         wa_ir_check TYPE mark,
         wa_mark     TYPE mark VALUE 'X'.

  TYPES : BEGIN OF ty1,
            ebeln LIKE ekbe-ebeln,
            ebelp LIKE ekbe-ebelp,
            bewtp LIKE ekbe-bewtp,
            menge LIKE ekbe-menge,
            dmbtr LIKE ekbe-dmbtr,
            shkzg LIKE ekbe-shkzg,
          END OF ty1.


  DATA : it1 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty1,
         it2 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty1,
         wa1 LIKE LINE OF it1.

  DATA : po_waers LIKE ekko-waers,
         po_bsart LIKE ekko-waers,
         co_waers LIKE ekko-waers.

*Begin of <RD1K967456>
  DATA : t_return TYPE TABLE OF bapireturn WITH HEADER LINE,
         wa_ret   LIKE LINE OF t_return.
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
***INITIAL CHECK
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
*
*CHECK BSEG-EBELN  IS NOT INITIAL.
*CHECK BSEG-EBELP IS NOT INITIAL.
*
*SELECT SINGLE waers bsart FROM ekko INTO (po_waers,po_bsart)
*WHERE ebeln = bseg-ebeln.
*
*SELECT SINGLE waers FROM t001 INTO co_waers WHERE bukrs
* = bseg-bukrs.
*
**First Check
**
**Logic 1
**
**Local Currency PO and DP in Local Currency
*
*If BSEG-PSWSL   = co_WAERS "[ Company code Currency ]
*And co_WAERS = po_WAERS. "[ For PO Currency ]
*
**Continue with Same logic as given
*
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
***INPUT DATA
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
**CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT
**PO NO
**PO LINE ITEM NO
*
*MOVE : BSEG-DMBTR TO W_DMBTR, "(CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT )
*       BSEG-EBELN TO W_EBELN, "(PO NO)
*       BSEG-EBELP TO W_EBELP. "(PO LINE ITEM NO)
*
*
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
***VALUE CALCULATION
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
**NOTE : VALUE NEED TO BE COLLECTED FROM FOLLOWING TWO TABLE
*
**EKPO
**EKBE
**
**FROM EKPO
*
*SELECT SINGLE
*NETWR "< NET ORDER VALUE IN PO CURRENCY >
*MENGE " < PURCHASE ORDER QUANTITY >
*NAVNW "< NON-DEDUCTIBLE INPUT TAX >
*FROM EKPO INTO
*  (W_NETWR,WPO_MENGE,W_NAVNW )
*WHERE EBELN = W_EBELN AND EBELP = W_EBELP. "= PO LINE ITEM VALUE " = PO LINE ITEM QUANTITY = PO LINE ITEM TAX VALUE
*
*
**FORM EKBE
*
*SELECT
*EBELN "<PO NO>
*EBELP "<PO LINE ITEM NO>
*BEWTP "< PURCHASE ORDER HISTORY CATEGORY >
*MENGE "< PURCHASE ORDER QUANTITY>
*DMBTR "< AMOUNT IN LOCAL CURRENCY >
*SHKZG "< DEBIT/CREDIT INDICATOR >
*FROM EKBE INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE IT1
*WHERE
*EBELN = W_EBELN AND EBELP = W_EBELP.
*
*
*
*
**CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT = [W_DMBTR]
**---------------------------------------------------------
**ALREADY PAID DOWN PAYMENT
*
**WAP_DMBTR = IT HAS TO BE CALCULATED FROM IT1 ADD ALL DMBTR FOR BEWTP = #A# DOWNPAYMENT AND SHKZG =
**H  CREDIT * -1
**S  DEBIT
*LOOP AT IT1 INTO WA1 WHERE BEWTP  = 'A'.
*IF WA1-SHKZG  = 'H'.
* WA1-DMBTR = WA1-DMBTR * -1.
*ENDIF.
* WAP_DMBTR = WAP_DMBTR + WA1-DMBTR.
*ENDLOOP.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**TOTAL DOWN PAYMENT = (CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT + ALREADY PAID DOWN PAYMENT )
*
*WDP_TOTAL = W_DMBTR + WAP_DMBTR.
**---------------------------------------------------------
** PO LINE ITEM VALUE = W_NETWR
**---------------------------------------------------------
** PO LINE ITEM TAX VALUE = W_NAVNW
**---------------------------------------------------------
** PO LINE ITEM QUANTITY = WPO_MENGE
**---------------------------------------------------------
**PO LINE ITEM TOTAL VALUE = (PO LINE ITEM VALUE+ PO LINE ITEM TAX VALUE)
*WPO_VALUE = W_NETWR + W_NAVNW .
**---------------------------------------------------------
**PO LINE ITEM RATE = PO LINE ITEM TOTAL VALUE / PO LINE ITEM QUANTITY
*
*WPO_RATE = WPO_VALUE / WPO_MENGE .
**---------------------------------------------------------
**EXCEED VALUE = TOTAL DOWN PAYMENT - PO LINE ITEM TOTAL VALUE
*
*WEX_VALUE = WDP_TOTAL - WPO_VALUE .
**---------------------------------------------------------
**GR QUANTITY
*
**WGR_MENGE = IT HAS TO BE CALCULATED FROM IT1 ADD ALL DMBTR FOR BEWTP = #E# GR AND SHKZG =
**H  CREDIT * -1
**S  DEBIT
*LOOP AT IT1 INTO WA1 WHERE BEWTP = 'E'.
*IF WA1-SHKZG  = 'H'.
* WA1-MENGE = WA1-MENGE * -1.
*ENDIF.
* WGR_MENGE = WGR_MENGE + WA1-MENGE.
*ENDLOOP.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**GR VALUE = GR QUANTITY * PO LINE ITEM RATE
*
*WGR_VALUE = WGR_MENGE * WPO_RATE.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**BALANCE QUANTITY = (PO LINE ITEM QUANTITY- GR QUANTITY)
*
*WBL_QTY = WPO_MENGE - WGR_MENGE.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**BALANCE VALUE = BALANCE QUANTITY * PO LINE ITEM RATE
*
*WBL_VALUE = WBL_QTY * WPO_RATE.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**IR = TRUE / FALSE
*
**IF
**BEWTP = Q IN IT1 TABLE, TRUE
**ELSE FALSE
*
*LOOP AT IT1 INTO WA1 WHERE BEWTP = 'Q'.
*WA_IR_CHECK = WA_MARK.
*ENDLOOP.
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
***CHECKING LOGIC FOR PO DOWNPAYMENT
***---------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**  STEP 1
*
**IF IR HAS BEEN MADE IR = TRUE
**WARNING: INVOICE RECEIPT HAS BEEN POSTED, PLEASE MAKE PAYMENT AGAINST INVOICE
**GO TO STEP 2
**ENDIF
**CREATE A MESSAGE WITH NO 008(&1 &2 &3 &4) IN MESSAGE CLASS(SE91) .
*IF WA_IR_CHECK = WA_MARK.
*  MESSAGE W008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'INVOICE RECEIPT HAS BEEN POSTED, PLEASE MAKE PAYMENT AGAINST INVOICE'(002).
*ENDIF.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**STEP 2
*
**CHECK GR QUANTITY > 0,
*IF WGR_MENGE > 0.
*
*IF
**CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT > BALANCE VALUE
*W_DMBTR > WBL_VALUE.
*MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED. DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT' W_DMBTR 'IS GREATER THAN ALLOWED VALUE' WBL_VALUE.
*ENDIF.
*ENDIF.
**ERROR: DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT #[CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT < W_DMBTR >]# > ALLOWED VALUE #[BALANCE VALUE< WBL_VALUE >]#. DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED
**ELSE
**---------------------------------------------------------
**GO TO STEP 3
**ENDIF
*
**STEP 3
*
*
**TOTAL DOWN PAYMENT > PO LINE ITEM TOTAL VALUE
*IF WDP_TOTAL > WPO_VALUE.
*MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED.DOWN PAYMENT EXCEED BY' WEX_VALUE 'THE TOTAL PO LINE ITEM VALUE' WPO_VALUE.
**ERROR : DOWN PAYMENT EXCEED BY #[EXCEED VALUE< WEX_VALUE >]# THE TOTAL PO LINE ITEM VALUE #[PO LINE ITEM TOTAL VALUE < WPO_VALUE >]#. DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED
*ELSE.
*ENDIF.
** End of < RD1K965595 >

* WGR_MENGE = WGR_MENGE + WA1-MENGE.
*ENDLOOP.

*
*WGR_VALUE = WGR_MENGE * WPO_RATE.
**---------------------------------------------------------

*
*WBL_QTY = WPO_MENGE - WGR_MENGE.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**BALANCE VALUE = BALANCE QUANTITY * PO LINE ITEM RATE
*
*WBL_VALUE = WBL_QTY * WPO_RATE.
**---------------------------------------------------------
**IR = TRUE / FALSE
*
**IF
**BEWTP = Q IN IT1 TABLE, TRUE
**ELSE FALSE
*
*LOOP AT IT1 INTO WA1 WHERE BEWTP = 'Q'.
*WA_IR_CHECK = WA_MARK.
*ENDLOOP.

**CHECK GR QUANTITY > 0,
*IF WGR_MENGE > 0.

**CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT > BALANCE VALUE
*W_DMBTR > WBL_VALUE.
*MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED. DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT' W_DMBTR 'IS GREATER THAN ALLOWED VALUE' WBL_VALUE.
*ENDIF.
*ENDIF.
**ERROR: DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT #[CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT < W_DMBTR >]# > ALLOWED VALUE #[BALANCE VALUE< WBL_VALUE >]#. DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED
**ELSE
**---------------------------------------------------------
**GO TO STEP 3
**ENDIF
*
**STEP 3

**ERROR : DOWN PAYMENT EXCEED BY #[EXCEED VALUE< WEX_VALUE >]# THE TOTAL PO LINE ITEM VALUE #[PO LINE ITEM TOTAL VALUE < WPO_VALUE >]#. DOWN PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED
*ELSE.
*ENDIF.

*ELSE.

**EKPO
**EKBE

**FROM EKPO

*EBELN "<PO NO>

*EBELN = W_EBELN AND EBELP = W_EBELP.

**CURRENT DOWN PAYMENT AMOUNT = [W_DMBTR]

   = bseg-bukrs.

*First Check
*Logic 1
*Local Currency PO and DP in Local Currency

  IF bseg-pswsl   = co_waers "[ Company code Currency ]
  AND co_waers = po_waers. "[ For PO Currency ]

    CALL FUNCTION 'ZFI_DP_CHECK'
      EXPORTING
        i_dmbtr  = bseg-dmbtr
        i_ebeln  = bseg-ebeln
        i_ebelp  = bseg-ebelp
      TABLES
        t_return = t_return.

    LOOP AT  t_return .
      IF  t_return-type = 'E'.
        MESSAGE e008(zfi_bapc) WITH  t_return-message.
      ELSEIF t_return-type = 'W'.
        MESSAGE w008(zfi_bapc) WITH t_return-message.
      ENDIF.

  ELSEIF bseg-pswsl  <> co_waers" [ Company code Currency ]

      ENDIF.

*Continue with Same logic as given
  ELSEIF
*  Logic 3
*Foreign Currency PO and DP in Local Currency

*If

  bseg-pswsl = co_waers "[ Company code Currency ]
  AND co_waers  <> po_waers." [ For PO Currency ]

*Convert the local currency in Foreign Currency and Compare

            wa_return  LIKE  bapiret1.

      bseg-dmbtr = bseg-dmbtr * wa_ex_rate-exch_rate."Exchange Rate and proceed with same logic
    ELSE.

*Get the Exchange Rate

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_EXCHANGERATE_GETDETAIL'
        EXPORTING
          rate_type  = 'CLT'
          from_curr  = po_waers
          to_currncy = bseg-pswsl

        i_ebelp  = bseg-ebelp

        i_dmbtr  = bseg-dmbtr

        return     = wa_return.

        EXPORTING
          rate_type  = 'CLT'
          from_curr  = po_waers
          to_currncy = bseg-pswsl

    ENDIF.

FORM uf211 CHANGING b_result.
  DATA :kostl       LIKE anlz-kostl,
        caufn       LIKE anlz-caufn,
        prctr       LIKE anlz-ps_psp_pnr2,
        wa_mark(1)  TYPE c,
        wa_check(1) TYPE c.

  MOVE : anlz-kostl TO kostl,
         anlz-caufn TO caufn,
         anlz-ps_psp_pnr2 TO prctr.

  """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""

  """""""commented by lipsy 23.02.2016 for removing error if more than one cost center in one line  RD1K9A000H

*  IF KOSTL IS NOT INITIAL.
*    IF CAUFN IS NOT INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'Enter only one cost object.'.
*    ENDIF.
*    IF PRCTR IS NOT INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'Enter only one cost object.'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF CAUFN IS NOT INITIAL.
*    IF  PRCTR IS NOT INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'Enter only one cost object.'.
*    ENDIF.
*    IF  KOSTL IS NOT INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'Enter only one cost object.'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF  PRCTR IS NOT INITIAL.
*    IF KOSTL   IS NOT INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'Enter only one cost object.'.
*    ENDIF.
*    IF  CAUFN IS NOT INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE E008(ZFI_BAPC) WITH 'Enter only one cost object.'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.

  """""""end of comment by lipsy 23.02.2016 for removing error if more than one cost center in one line  RD1K9A000H
  """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
*Begin of <RD1K965595>
  IF prctr IS INITIAL AND kostl IS INITIAL AND caufn IS INITIAL

    """""""""""""""""""""""
    "added by lipsy 23.02.2016  RD1K9A000H
    AND anlz-imkey IS INITIAL.
    "end of addition by lipsy 23.02.2016  RD1K9A000H
    """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
    MESSAGE e008(zfi_bapc) WITH 'Enter any one of the cost obects'.
  ENDIF.

FORM uf212 CHANGING b_result.
*TABLES T001B_PS_PER,tka02,cepc
  DATA : w_kokrs LIKE tka02-kokrs.
  SELECT SINGLE kokrs INTO w_kokrs FROM tka02 WHERE bukrs = bkpf-bukrs.
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.

*Begin of RD1K964660

  DATA : wa_bkont TYPE prctr,
         wa_ye2   TYPE char4,
         wa_pe2   TYPE char3,
         wa_toye2 TYPE char4,
         wa_tope2 TYPE char3.

" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*  SELECT SINGLE  bkont frye2 frpe2 toye2
*tope2 FROM t001b_ps_per INTO (wa_bkont,wa_ye2,wa_pe2,wa_toye2,wa_tope2 )
*                                            WHERE bkont = bseg-prctr.
 SELECT BKONT FRYE2 FRPE2 TOYE2 TOPE2 FROM T001B_PS_PER   UP TO 1 ROWS
INTO (WA_BKONT , WA_YE2 , WA_PE2 , WA_TOYE2 , WA_TOPE2 ) WHERE BKONT =
BSEG-PRCTR  ORDER BY  RRCTY BUKRS FIELD BKONT .   ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC

  IF sy-subrc = 0.

    IF ( bkpf-gjahr BETWEEN wa_ye2 AND wa_toye2 ) AND ( bkpf-monat BETWEEN wa_pe2+1(2) AND wa_tope2+1(2) ).

    ELSE.
      MESSAGE e009(zfi_bapc) WITH bkpf-monat  bseg-prctr .

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

*End of RD1K964660




ENDFORM.                                                    "uf212

FORM uf214 CHANGING b_result.
  DATA wa_gsber TYPE gsber.
  IF anlz-caufn IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE gsber INTO wa_gsber FROM aufk
            WHERE aufnr = anlz-caufn.
  ENDIF.
  IF anlz-ps_psp_pnr2 IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE pgsbr FROM prps INTO wa_gsber
       WHERE pspnr = anlz-ps_psp_pnr2.
  ENDIF.
  IF anlz-kostl IS NOT INITIAL.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*    SELECT SINGLE gsber FROM csks INTO wa_gsber
*      WHERE kostl = anlz-kostl.
 SELECT GSBER FROM CSKS   UP TO 1 ROWS INTO WA_GSBER WHERE KOSTL =
ANLZ-KOSTL  ORDER BY  KOKRS KOSTL DATBI .   ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
  ENDIF.
  IF wa_gsber <> anlz-gsber.
    MESSAGE  e008(zfi_bapc) WITH 'Business Area should be' wa_gsber.
  ENDIF.
  IF anlz-gsber IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE  e008(zfi_bapc) WITH 'Business Area should not be initial'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                                                    "uf214
*End of <RD1K965816>
