FORM uf215 CHANGING b_result.
  DATA wa_status TYPE zfisch23status1-status.
  DATA it_status TYPE TABLE OF zfisch23status1 WITH HEADER LINE.
*SELECT SINGLE  status FROM ZFISCH23STATUS1 INTO wa_status
*  WHERE BUKRS = bkpf-BUKRS AND GJAHR = bkpf-GJAHR AND MONAT = bkpf-MONAT.
*Begin of  <RD1K968450>
  DATA : wa_date TYPE sy-datum,
         wa_time TYPE uzeit.
*SELECT SINGLE  * FROM ZFISCH23STATUS1 INTO it_status
*  WHERE BUKRS = bkpf-BUKRS AND GJAHR = bkpf-GJAHR AND MONAT = bkpf-MONAT.
*IF sy-subrc = 0.
*SORT it_status by EDATE time.
*READ TABLE it_status index 1.
*wa_status = it_status-status.
  SELECT SINGLE MAX( edate ) FROM zfisch23status1 INTO (wa_date)
 WHERE bukrs = bkpf-bukrs AND gjahr = bkpf-gjahr AND monat = bkpf-monat.

*select SINGLE MAX( TIME ) STATUS from zfisch23status1 into (wa_TIME,WA_STATUS)
  SELECT SINGLE MAX( time ) FROM zfisch23status1 INTO (wa_time)
  WHERE bukrs = bkpf-bukrs AND gjahr = bkpf-gjahr AND monat = bkpf-monat
  AND edate = wa_date .

*  SELECT SINGLE status FROM zfisch23status1 INTO (wa_status)

 SY-UNAME AND SUBTY = '0001' AND BEGDA <= SY-DATUM AND ENDDA >=
SY-DATUM  ORDER BY  PERNR SUBTY OBJPS SPRPS ENDDA BEGDA SEQNR .

    ENDIF.

            AND subty = '01'
            AND begda => back_date
            AND ( endda < sy-datum OR endda = '99991231' ).

* appendind them into a single list ist_user_bukrs
    LOOP AT ist_pa0027 INTO wa_pa0027.

*end
      count = 1.
      DO 25 TIMES.
        CLEAR count_c.

        SHIFT count_c RIGHT DELETING TRAILING space.
        OVERLAY count_c WITH '00'.

        CONCATENATE'WA_PA0027-KPR' count_c INTO str_kpr. " WA_PA0027-KPR01... WA_PA0027-KPR25
        CONCATENATE'WA_PA0027-KBU' count_c INTO str_comp. " WA_PA0027-KBU01... WA_PA0027-KBU25

FORM uf222 CHANGING b_result.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_table> TYPE ANY TABLE .
  DATA: it_bseg     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg,
        wa_bseg     TYPE bseg,
        wa_bseg1    TYPE bseg,
        if_text(40) TYPE c,
        l_trans     TYPE zfi_osbal_trans.
  DATA : ist_setleaf_gl TYPE TABLE OF setleaf,
         wa_setleaf_gl  TYPE setleaf.

        l_pernr1 TYPE persno.

  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  it_bseg[] = <fs_table>.

                                    WHERE userid = sy-uname AND
                                             begda LE sy-datum AND
                                             endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    READ TABLE it_bseg INTO wa_bseg WITH KEY hkont = '0000100210'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      REFRESH ist_setleaf_gl.
      SELECT * FROM setleaf
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf_gl
          WHERE setclass = '0000'
            AND setname = 'HKONT_OUTSTANBAL'.

      LOOP AT it_bseg INTO wa_bseg1 WHERE hkont NE wa_bseg-hkont.
        READ TABLE ist_setleaf_gl INTO wa_setleaf_gl
                            WITH KEY valfrom = wa_bseg1-hkont.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          MESSAGE e625(zfi) .

        ENDIF.
        IF wa_bseg1-koart = 'K'.

          l_pernr = 7000000 - wa_bseg1-lifnr.

*        SELECT SINGLE pernr FROM pa0000 INTO l_pernr1 WHERE
*                                     pernr = l_pernr AND
*                                     begda LE sy-datum AND
*                                     endda GE sy-datum AND
*                                     stat2 = '0'.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          SELECT SINGLE pernr FROM pa0302 INTO l_pernr1 WHERE
                                       pernr = l_pernr AND
                                    ( massn = '11' OR massn = 'S1' ) AND
                                    massg = '03'.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            MESSAGE e100(zfi) WITH 'Employee is not sepearated due to demise'

*        ENDIF.
        ENDIF.




      ENDLOOP.

FORM uf223 CHANGING b_result.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_table> TYPE ANY TABLE .
  DATA: it_agr_users       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF agr_users,
        wa_agr_users       TYPE agr_users,
        it_zfi_gl_role_chk TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zfi_gl_role_chk,
        wa_zfi_gl_role_chk TYPE zfi_gl_role_chk.

  DATA : ist_setleaf_gl TYPE TABLE OF setleaf,
         wa_setleaf_gl  TYPE setleaf.

  REFRESH ist_setleaf_gl.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM setleaf INTO  wa_setleaf_gl
  WHERE setclass = '0000'
    AND setname = 'ZFI_GL_ROLE_EXEMPTION'
    AND valfrom = sy-uname.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    SELECT * FROM agr_users INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_agr_users WHERE uname = sy-uname
                               AND to_dat >= sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      SELECT  * FROM zfi_gl_role_chk INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_zfi_gl_role_chk
                                 WHERE hkontfrom <= bseg-hkont AND
                                       hkontto  >= bseg-hkont.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        LOOP AT it_zfi_gl_role_chk INTO wa_zfi_gl_role_chk.
          READ TABLE it_agr_users INTO wa_agr_users WITH KEY agr_name = wa_zfi_gl_role_chk-agr_name.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
            MESSAGE e105(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                                                    "uf223
*End of <RD1K976816> CAB_SUDHIR                                                  "uf223

*Begin of <RD1K978595> CAB_PAREEK
FORM uf224 CHANGING b_result.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_table> TYPE ANY TABLE .
  DATA: it_bseg     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg,
        wa_bseg     TYPE bseg,
        wa_bseg1    TYPE bseg,
        if_text(40) TYPE c.
  DATA : ist_setleaf_gl  TYPE TABLE OF setleaf,
         wa_setleaf_gl   TYPE setleaf,
         ist_setleaf_gl1 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf,
         wa_setleaf_gl1  TYPE setleaf.
  DATA: l_check TYPE c.

  if_text = '(SAPMF05A)XBSEG[]' .        " MF05AFV0_VALIDATION_BELEG
  ASSIGN (if_text) TO  <fs_table> .
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  it_bseg[] = <fs_table>.

  REFRESH ist_setleaf_gl.
  SELECT * FROM setleaf
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf_gl
      WHERE setclass = '0000'
        AND setname = 'HKONT_TAX'.

  CLEAR l_check.
  LOOP AT it_bseg INTO wa_bseg.
    READ TABLE ist_setleaf_gl INTO wa_setleaf_gl
                        WITH KEY valfrom = wa_bseg-hkont.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      l_check = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF l_check = 'X'.
    REFRESH ist_setleaf_gl1.
    SELECT * FROM setleaf
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf_gl1
        WHERE setclass = '0000'
          AND setname = 'FIPOS_TAX'.
    LOOP AT it_bseg INTO wa_bseg.
      READ TABLE ist_setleaf_gl1 INTO wa_setleaf_gl1
                          WITH KEY valfrom = wa_bseg-fipos.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        READ TABLE ist_setleaf_gl INTO wa_setleaf_gl
                        WITH KEY valfrom = wa_bseg-hkont.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          MESSAGE e423(zfi) WITH  wa_bseg-hkont.

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                                                    "uf224

    l_bukrs = proj-vbukr.
    l_ba = proj-vgsbr.
    l_prctr = proj-prctr.
    CLEAR wa_val.
*    if l_posid = 'DL'.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zprpswbsval INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF wa_val

* Begin RD1K979242 CAB_PAREEK  PS_WBS_exit for  CR 30006762
FORM uf228  CHANGING b_result.
  DATA:  it_val  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zpswbscode,
         wa_val  TYPE zpswbscode,
         d_plen  TYPE i,
         t_plen  TYPE i,
         g_found TYPE c,
         l_coun  TYPE i,
         l_posid TYPE ps_posid.
  CLEAR l_posid.
  l_posid = prps-posid.
  CONDENSE l_posid NO-GAPS.
  REFRESH it_val[].
  SELECT  * FROM zpswbscode INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_val
                       WHERE profl = proj-profl AND
                             stufe = prps-stufe.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    d_plen = strlen( l_posid ).
    LOOP AT it_val INTO wa_val.
      g_found = 'X'.
      CLEAR l_coun.
      t_plen = strlen( wa_val-pspnr ).
      IF d_plen = t_plen.
        DO t_plen TIMES.
          IF wa_val-pspnr+l_coun(1) NE '+'.
            IF wa_val-pspnr+l_coun(1) NE l_posid+l_coun(1).
              CLEAR g_found.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
          l_coun = l_coun + 1.
        ENDDO.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR g_found.
      ENDIF.
      IF g_found = 'X'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF g_found IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e000(zps) WITH 'Please enter valid WBS Code'.
    ENDIF.

* Begin RD1K979642 CAB_PAREEK  PS_WBS_exit for  CR 30006873
FORM uf230  CHANGING b_result.
  DATA:  l_pspid        TYPE ps_pspid,
         l_posid        TYPE ps_posid,
         l_len          TYPE i,
         l_objnr        LIKE prps-objnr,
         l_jest         TYPE jest,

         wa_setleaf_gl  TYPE setleaf.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM setleaf
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF wa_setleaf_gl
      WHERE setclass = '0000'
        AND setname = 'ZJV_VALIDATION'
        AND valfrom = proj-pspid.

  IF sy-subrc NE 0.

    CLEAR l_pspid.

    IF NOT proj-pspid IS INITIAL.
      l_pspid = proj-pspid.
      CONDENSE l_pspid NO-GAPS.

      IF NOT proj-vname IS INITIAL.
        IF l_pspid+2(2) NE proj-vname+2(2).
          MESSAGE e626(zfi).
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF NOT proj-prctr IS INITIAL.
        IF l_pspid+2(2) NE proj-prctr+5(2).
          MESSAGE e626(zfi).
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF NOT prps-posid IS INITIAL AND prps-usr00 NE 'LKD'.
      CLEAR l_objnr.
      SELECT SINGLE objnr INTO l_objnr FROM prps WHERE posid = prps-posid.
      CLEAR l_jest.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM jest INTO l_jest
                      WHERE objnr = l_objnr AND stat = 'I0043'
                                      AND inact NE 'X'.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        l_posid = prps-posid.
        CONDENSE l_posid NO-GAPS.
        IF NOT prps-vname IS INITIAL.
          IF l_posid+2(2) NE prps-vname+2(2).
            MESSAGE e626(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF NOT prps-prctr IS INITIAL.
          IF l_posid+2(2) NE prps-prctr+5(2).
            MESSAGE e626(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR l_pspid.
        l_pspid = proj-pspid.
        CONDENSE l_pspid NO-GAPS.

        IF NOT proj-pspid IS INITIAL.
          IF l_pspid+2(2) NE l_posid+2(2).
            MESSAGE e626(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF NOT prps-akstl IS INITIAL.
          IF l_posid+2(2) NE prps-akstl+5(2).
            MESSAGE e626(zfi).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                                                    "UF230

*begin RD1K983296       CAB_ALOK   Exchange rate deviation through Exit - CR 30008457
FORM uf237 CHANGING b_result.

  DATA: perc TYPE t001-waabw.

* begin RD1K984042 CAB_ALOK CR 30008817: Exclusion of (Exchange Gain/loss line item) Auto generated lines
* Forex gain/loss line item contains  bseg-dmbtr = Amount in INR (Exchange gain/loss Amt), But bseg-wrbtr = 0 USD
* this causes dump, so need to be excluded.
  IF bseg-xauto = ''. " 'X': auto-genrated line item.
* end RD1K984042 CAB_ALOK CR 30008817: Exclusion of (Exchange Gain/loss line item) Auto generated lines

* Exclude FBV2,FBV3,FB03,FB08,FBRA,FB02
** Begin RD1K983474  Addl 1
    IF sy-tcode <> 'FB02'       " Not to be run for these Tcodes
*  and  sy-tcode <> 'FBV2'
         AND  sy-tcode <> 'FBV3'
            AND  sy-tcode <> 'FB03'
               AND  sy-tcode <> 'FB08'
                   AND  sy-tcode <> 'FBRA'.
** end RD1K983474 Addl 1
** get tolerance limit from system
      SELECT SINGLE waabw
        FROM t001
          INTO perc
        WHERE bukrs = bkpf-bukrs.

      DATA: dmbtr_upper_limit TYPE bseg-dmbtr,
** Begin RD1K983474 Addl 1
*      calc_rate type  bkpf-KURSF . " dec 9,5 => Overflow
            calc_rate         TYPE f.
      DATA: dmbtr_lower_limit TYPE bseg-dmbtr.
** End  RD1K983474 Addl 1

** calculate upper limit of allowed amount (DMBTR, INR)
      dmbtr_upper_limit = ( bseg-wrbtr * bkpf-kursf ) * ( 100 + perc ) / 100.

** Begin RD1K983474 Addl 1
** calculate lower limit
      dmbtr_lower_limit = ( bseg-wrbtr * bkpf-kursf ) * ( 100 - perc ) / 100.
** End  RD1K983474 Addl 1

** calculate exchange rate as per input data
**if bseg-wrbtr is NOT INITIAL.   "Avoid Div by 0
      calc_rate = bseg-dmbtr / bseg-wrbtr.          " INR / USD

      IF bkpf-waers <> 'INR'.

** Begin RD1K983474 Addl 1
*    if BSEG-DMBtr > DMBTR_UPPER_LIMIT.
        IF bseg-dmbtr > dmbtr_upper_limit
          OR bseg-dmbtr < dmbtr_lower_limit.
** End  RD1K983474 Addl 1

FORM uf240 CHANGING b_result.
  DATA : wa_setleaf TYPE setleaf.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_table4> TYPE ANY TABLE .
  DATA: if_text4(40) TYPE c,
        it_bseg4     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg,
        wa_bseg4     TYPE bseg.
  DATA: v_dec  TYPE p DECIMALS 3,
        v_temp TYPE char20,
        v1     TYPE char10,
        v2     TYPE  char10,
        v_len  TYPE i.

  DATA : wa_setleaf_240 TYPE setleaf.

  CLEAR: if_text4,it_bseg4,wa_bseg4.
  REFRESH it_bseg4.

  if_text4 = '(SAPMF05A)XBSEG[]' .        " MF05AFV0_VALIDATION_BELEG
  ASSIGN (if_text4) TO  <fs_table4> .
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  it_bseg4[] = <fs_table4>.

  LOOP AT it_bseg4 INTO wa_bseg4
     WHERE buzei <> wa_bseg4-buzei
    AND koart = 'D'
    AND hkont = '0000091268' .

    CLEAR:len, v_temp,v1,v2.

    v_temp   = wa_bseg4-dmbtr.
    CONDENSE v_temp .
    SPLIT  v_temp AT  '.'  INTO v1 v2 .

    CONDENSE v2  NO-GAPS.
    IF v2 > 0.
      len = strlen( v2 ).
    ENDIF.
    IF wa_bseg4-dmbtr > 50000 .
      MESSAGE e425(zfi) WITH text-305.
    ENDIF.

    IF  len > 0.
      MESSAGE e425(zfi) WITH text-306.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                                                    "UF240

FORM u247 USING b_result.

  DATA:  dt_part  TYPE char2,
         e_date   TYPE datum,
         p_period LIKE bkpf-monat,
         dt_int   TYPE int4.
  DATA: BEGIN OF wa_bkpf,
          bukrs   LIKE bkpf-bukrs,
          p_date  LIKE bkpf-budat,
          p_monat LIKE bkpf-monat,

        END OF wa_bkpf.
  DATA: it_bkpf LIKE TABLE OF wa_bkpf.

  DATA: it_set  TYPE TABLE OF rgsb4,
        it_set1 TYPE TABLE OF rgsb4,
        wa_set  LIKE LINE OF it_set.

  DATA: v_check TYPE c VALUE ''.

  dt_part = sy-datum+6(2).
  dt_int = dt_part.


  CALL FUNCTION 'G_SET_GET_ALL_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      client        = ''
      setnr         = 'ZTDS_GL_RESTRIC'
      table         = 'BSEG'
      class         = '0000'
      fieldname     = 'HKONT'
    TABLES
      set_values    = it_set
    EXCEPTIONS
      set_not_found = 1.

  CALL FUNCTION 'G_SET_GET_ALL_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      client        = ''
      setnr         = 'ZTDS_TAXCODE_RESTRIC'
      table         = 'BSEG'
      class         = '0000'
      fieldname     = 'MWSKZ'
    TABLES
      set_values    = it_set1
    EXCEPTIONS
      set_not_found = 1.


  IF dt_int < 7. "if current date is less than 7 then get previous month else get system date.


    CALL FUNCTION 'OIL_GET_PREV_MONTH'
      EXPORTING
        i_date = sy-datum
      IMPORTING
        e_date = e_date.
  ELSE.
    e_date = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  e_date+6(2) = 01. "Setting date as 1st for the month

  LOOP AT it_set INTO wa_set.


    IF bseg-hkont EQ wa_set-from.

      IF bkpf-budat < e_date.

        MESSAGE 'Tax period is closed for posting period ' TYPE 'E'.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
    CLEAR wa_set.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT it_set1 INTO wa_set.


    IF bseg-mwskz EQ wa_set-from.

      IF bkpf-budat < e_date.

        MESSAGE 'Tax period is closed for posting period ' TYPE 'E'.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
    CLEAR wa_set.
  ENDLOOP.


*
*ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                                                    "u247

**---------------------------------------------------------------------
*
**       FORM U248
**---------------------------------------------------------------------
* GL period closing
**---------------------------------------------------------------------
FORM u248 USING b_result.

  DATA: sy_mon    TYPE i,
        pd_mon    TYPE i,
        last_date TYPE datum,
        last_mon  TYPE datum,
        err_msg   TYPE char80.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_gl_closure INTO @DATA(wa_glc) WHERE bukrs = @bkpf-bukrs AND hkont = @bseg-hkont.
  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR: sy_mon, pd_mon, err_msg.

    IF wa_glc-closure = 'MM'.
      sy_mon = sy-datum+4(2).
      pd_mon = bkpf-budat+4(2) + 1.

      IF sy_mon GT pd_mon.
        CONCATENATE 'Posting is closed for GL acc. -' bseg-hkont INTO err_msg SEPARATED BY space.
        MESSAGE err_msg TYPE 'E'.

      ELSEIF sy_mon EQ pd_mon AND sy-datum+6(2) GT '15'.
        CONCATENATE 'Posting is closed for GL acc. -' bseg-hkont INTO err_msg SEPARATED BY space.
        MESSAGE err_msg TYPE 'E'.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF wa_glc-closure = 'ME'.
      sy_mon = sy-datum+4(2).
      pd_mon = bkpf-budat+4(2) + 1.

      IF sy_mon GT pd_mon .
        CONCATENATE 'Posting is closed for GL acc. -' bseg-hkont INTO err_msg SEPARATED BY space.
        MESSAGE err_msg TYPE 'E'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                                                    "u248

           blart LIKE bkpf-blart,
         END OF ist_blart_nolimit.

           blart LIKE bkpf-blart,
         END OF ist_blart_limit10.

  DATA : ist_setleaf1 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf .
  DATA : wa_ist_setleaf1 TYPE  setleaf.
  DATA : ist_setleaf2 TYPE TABLE OF setleaf .
  DATA : wa_ist_setleaf2 TYPE  setleaf.

  SELECT * FROM  setleaf
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_setleaf1
WHERE  setname  = 'BDP_DOCTYPE_NOLIMIT_OVC'   " Doc types for DEP RUN etc, No financial limits
AND  setclass = '0000'.

  REFRESH ist_blart_nolimit.
  LOOP AT ist_setleaf1 INTO wa_ist_setleaf1 .
    ist_blart_nolimit-blart = wa_ist_setleaf1-valfrom.
    APPEND ist_blart_nolimit.

      AND  setclass = '0000'.

  REFRESH ist_blart_limit10.
  LOOP AT ist_setleaf2 INTO wa_ist_setleaf2 .
    ist_blart_limit10-blart = wa_ist_setleaf2-valfrom.
    APPEND ist_blart_limit10.
    CLEAR wa_ist_setleaf2.
  ENDLOOP.

FORM uf246 USING b_result.

  DATA: wa_zmm_vms_cr_new TYPE zmm_vms_cr_new,
        wa_pa0001         TYPE pa0001,
        flag,
        vend,
        spgl              TYPE bseg-umskz.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_bseg> TYPE table.

  DATA: it_bseg TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg.
  DATA wa_bseg TYPE bseg.
  IF bkpf-stblg IS INITIAL.
    ASSIGN ('(SAPMF05A)XBSEG[]') TO <fs_bseg>.

    CHECK <fs_bseg>  IS ASSIGNED.
    MOVE <fs_bseg>[] TO it_bseg.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpa0001 INTO wa_pa0001 WHERE pernr  =  sy-uname      AND
                                                        sprps  = ' '            AND
                                                        endda  >= sy-datum        AND
                                                        begda <= sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      flag = 'X'.
    ENDIF.

" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_vms_cr_new INTO wa_zmm_vms_cr_new WHERE core_user_id = sy-uname.
 SELECT * FROM ZMM_VMS_CR_NEW   UP TO 1 ROWS INTO WA_ZMM_VMS_CR_NEW
WHERE CORE_USER_ID = SY-UNAME  ORDER BY  CORE_USER_ID CPF_NO .
ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpa0001 INTO wa_pa0001 WHERE pernr =  wa_zmm_vms_cr_new-cpf_no AND

                                                       begda <= sy-datum.

      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        flag = 'X'.
      ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT it_bseg INTO wa_bseg WHERE koart = 'K'.
      vend = 'X'.
      spgl = wa_bseg-umskz.
    ENDLOOP.

FORM uf247 USING b_result.

  DATA:vend.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_bseg> TYPE table.

  DATA: it_bseg TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg.
  DATA wa_bseg TYPE bseg.
  IF bkpf-stblg IS INITIAL.
    ASSIGN ('(SAPMF05A)XBSEG[]') TO <fs_bseg>.

    CHECK <fs_bseg>  IS ASSIGNED.
    MOVE <fs_bseg>[] TO it_bseg.

    LOOP AT it_bseg INTO wa_bseg WHERE koart = 'K'.
      vend = 'X'.
    ENDLOOP.

    IF vend = 'X'.
      MESSAGE 'Vendor document should be posted with correct document type' TYPE 'E'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM u256 USING b_result.

  IF bkpf-waers IS NOT INITIAL.
    DATA: it_zkblnr TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg.
    DATA wa_zkblnr TYPE bseg.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_zkblnr> TYPE table.
    ASSIGN ('(SAPMF05A)XBSEG[]') TO <fs_zkblnr>.
    CHECK <fs_zkblnr>  IS ASSIGNED.
    MOVE <fs_zkblnr>[] TO it_zkblnr.

    IF it_zkblnr IS NOT INITIAL.
      LOOP AT it_zkblnr INTO wa_zkblnr WHERE kblnr IS NOT INITIAL.
        DATA(zkblnr) =  wa_zkblnr-kblnr.
        EXIT.
      ENDLOOP.

      IF zkblnr IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT SINGLE belnr,waers
        FROM kblk
        INTO @DATA(lv_waers)
        WHERE belnr = @zkblnr
        AND bukrs = 'OVL'
        AND kokrs = 'OVL'
        AND fikrs = 'OVL'.

        IF lv_waers-waers IS NOT INITIAL AND bkpf-waers NE lv_waers-waers.
          MESSAGE e911(zfi).
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM uf249 USING b_result.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
  DATA : lv_pan TYPE LFA1-j_1ipanno.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
  DATA : lt_taxcd TYPE TABLE OF with_item.
  DATA : lv_lifnr TYPE lifnr.
  DATA : lv_lifnr_1 TYPE lifnr.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_taxcd> TYPE table.
  ASSIGN ('(SAPLFWTD)XWITH[]') TO <fs_taxcd>.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    lt_taxcd = <fs_taxcd>.
  ENDIF.


  IF lv_lifnr_1 IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_lifnr = lv_lifnr_1.
  ELSE.
    lv_lifnr = bseg-lifnr.
  ENDIF.

  IF lv_lifnr IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR lv_pan.
    " Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026  FOR ATC
*    SELECT SINGLE j_1ipanno
*      FROM j_1imovend
*        INTO lv_pan
*          WHERE lifnr = lv_lifnr.
    SELECT SINGLE j_1ipanno
      FROM lfa1
        INTO lv_pan
          WHERE lifnr = lv_lifnr.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC
    IF lv_pan IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF lv_pan+3(1) = 'P' OR  lv_pan+3(1) = 'H'.
        READ TABLE lt_taxcd INTO DATA(ls_taxcd) WITH KEY witht = '65'.
        IF ls_taxcd-wt_withcd IS NOT INITIAL AND ls_taxcd-wt_withcd NE 'C1' .
          CLEAR ls_taxcd.
          MESSAGE e672(zfi).
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR ls_taxcd.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR ls_taxcd.
        READ TABLE lt_taxcd INTO ls_taxcd WITH KEY wt_withcd = 'C1' witht = '65'.
        IF ls_taxcd-wt_withcd = 'C1'.
          MESSAGE e546(zfi).
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.

 SELECT BLART FROM BKPF   UP TO 1 ROWS INTO @DATA(VBKPF_BLART) WHERE
BELNR = @BKPF-BELNR  ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .   ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 05.06.2026 FOR ATC

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF vbkpf_blart = 'CB' OR vbkpf_blart = 'OS'.
      MESSAGE 'Do not clear document with document type CB or OS' TYPE 'E'.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.
*ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM u260 USING b_result.

*  SELECT * FROM zjv_doc INTO TABLE @DATA(it_doc) WHERE user_name = @sy-uname .
  SELECT * FROM  zjv_delegate INTO TABLE @DATA(it_doc) WHERE user_name = @sy-uname  and to_date le @sy-datum.

  LOOP AT it_doc INTO DATA(wa_doc).
    READ TABLE it_doc INTO wa_doc  WITH KEY joint_venture = bseg-vname.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CONCATENATE 'You are not authorized for Parking/Posting in Venture-'  bseg-vname INTO DATA(lv_msg).
      MESSAGE lv_msg  TYPE 'E' DISPLAY LIKE 'I'.

    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.
